12.4.18 以速度 v 运动的 $\pi^{0}$ 介子(静止质量为 m) 裂变为两个相同的 $\gamma$ 量子, 试确定 $\gamma$ 量子的飞散角.

解 方法一: 设 p 为 $\pi^{0}$ 介子的动量, $p_{1}, p_{2}$ 分别为两个 $\gamma$ 量子的动量, 裂变过程如图 12.31 所示. 用 $E^{2}-c^{2}p^{2}$ 在裂变前后对任何惯性参考系均为不变量.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/71df54ee-e286-495d-894a-26de614f5c7d/4cf56fec5d0476f79b6fa9ae52f50c3152e1d42b6491996c159154aad11a0d12.jpg)



图12.31


裂变前用 $\pi^{0}$ 介子静止参考系, 裂变后用实验室参考系. 设光子即 $\gamma$ 量子的能量为 E, 则 $p_{1}=p_{2}=\frac{E}{c}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (m c ^ {2}) ^ {2} & = (2 E) ^ {2} - c ^ {2} (\pmb {p} _ {1} + \pmb {p} _ {2}) \cdot (\pmb {p} _ {1} + \pmb {p} _ {2}) \\ & = 4 E ^ {2} - c ^ {2} (p _ {1} ^ {2} + p _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 p _ {1} p _ {2} \cos \theta) \\ & = 2 E ^ {2} (1 - \cos \theta) = 4 E ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \frac {\theta}{2} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

由能量守恒,每个光子的能量等于 $\pi^{0}$ 介子的能量的一半,

$$
E = \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {m c ^ {2}}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}\tag{2}
$$

将式(2)代入式(1)，即得

$$
\sin^ {2} \frac {\theta}{2} = 1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}
$$

$$
\theta = 2 \arcsin \sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}
$$

方法二:由能量和动量守恒,

$$
E = \frac {1}{2} \frac {m c ^ {2}}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}\tag{3}
$$

$$
\frac {m v}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}} = (p _ {1} + p _ {2}) \cos \frac {\theta}{2}\tag{4}
$$

$$
p _ {1} \sin \frac {\theta}{2} = p _ {2} \sin \frac {\theta}{2}
$$

写上述关系时,已考虑产生的两个 $\gamma$ 量子是相同的,能量均为 E,动量大小相同.

将 $p_1 = p_2 = \frac{E}{c}$ 代入式(4). 并用式(1)消去 $E$ , 得

$$
\frac {m v}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}} = \frac {m c}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}} \cos \frac {\theta}{2}
$$

所以

$$
\cos \frac {\theta}{2} = \frac {v}{c}, \quad \sin \frac {\theta}{2} = \sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}
$$

$$
\theta = 2 \arccos \left({\frac {v}{c}}\right) \quad {\text {或}} \quad 2 \arcsin {\sqrt {1 - \left({\frac {v}{c}}\right) ^ {2}}}
$$

12.4.19 用真空中电磁波的相位是洛伦兹变换下的不变量,导出多普勒效应的下列两个式子:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \nu = \sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}} \frac {c}{c - v \cos \theta} \nu^ {\prime} \\ \nu = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}} \frac {c + v \cos \theta^ {\prime}}{c} \nu^ {\prime} \end{array}
$$

解 本题写的四维矢量与以前各题的写法有所不同：

四维位置矢量写为 $(x,y,z,\mathrm{ict})$ ;

四维动量矢量写为 $(p_{x}, p_{y}, p_{z}, \mathrm{i} \frac{E}{c})$ ;

四维波矢量写为 $(k_x, k_y, k_z, \mathrm{i} \frac{\omega}{c})$ .

这样写的好处是四维矢量的长度是不变量，例如 $x^{2} + y^{2} + z^{2} + (\mathrm{i}ct)^{2} = x^{2} + y^{2} + z^{2} - (ct)^{2},$ $p_x^2 + p_y^2 + p_z^2 - \left(\frac{E}{c}\right)^2$ 即 $c^2 p^2 - E^2$ 或 $E^2 - c^2 p^2$ 为不变量， $k_x^2 + k_y^2 + k_z^2 - \left(\frac{\omega}{c}\right)^2 = k^2 - \left(\frac{\omega}{c}\right)^2 = 0$ 为不变量，而且两个四维矢量的标积也是不变量，例如

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & k _ {x} x + k _ {y} y + k _ {z} z + (\mathrm{i} c t) (\mathrm{i} \frac {\omega}{c}) \\ & = k _ {x} x + k _ {y} y + k _ {z} z - \omega t = \boldsymbol {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {r} - \omega t \end{array}
$$

为不变量,因此真空中电磁波的相位是洛伦兹变换下的不变量.

现考虑在 $S$ 系中有一电磁波在 $xy$ 平面内从原点沿与 $x$ 轴夹角为 $\theta$ 的方向传播，设在 $O$ 点时，电磁波的初相位为零。则在 $t$ 时刻位于 $P$ 点，相位为 $\omega t - kr$ ，其中 $r$ 是 $P$ 点离原点的距离。 $P$ 点的坐标 $x, y$ 为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & x = r \cos \theta , \quad y = r \sin \theta \\ & r = r (\cos^ {2} \theta + \sin^ {2} \theta) = x \cos \theta + y \sin \theta \end{array}
$$

由相位是洛伦兹变换下的不变量，

$$
\omega t - k r = \omega^ {\prime} t ^ {\prime} - k ^ {\prime} r ^ {\prime}
$$

因

$$
\omega = 2 \pi \nu , k = \frac {2 \pi}{\lambda} = \frac {2 \pi \nu}{c}
$$

$$
2 \pi \nu (t - \frac {1}{c} x \cos \theta - \frac {1}{c} y \sin \theta)
$$

$$
= 2 x y ^ {\prime} \left(t ^ {\prime} - \frac {1}{c} x ^ {\prime} \cos \theta^ {\prime} - \frac {1}{c} y ^ {\prime} \sin \theta^ {\prime}\right)
$$

将洛伦兹变换

$$
x ^ {\prime} = \frac {x - v t}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}, \quad y ^ {\prime} = y, \quad t ^ {\prime} = \frac {t - \frac {v}{c ^ {2}} x}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}
$$

代入上式等号右边，得

$$
2 \pi \nu^ {\prime} \left[ \frac {t - \frac {v}{c ^ {2}} x}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}} - \frac {1}{c} \frac {x - v t}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}} \cos \theta^ {\prime} - \frac {1}{c} y \sin \theta^ {\prime} \right]
$$

与等号左边比较，两边 $t, x, y$ 的系数应分别相等，得

$$
\nu = \nu^ {\prime} \frac {1 + \frac {v}{c} \cos \theta^ {\prime}}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}\tag{1}
$$

$$
\nu \cos \theta = \nu^ {\prime} \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}} (\frac {v}{c} + \cos \theta^ {\prime})\tag{2}
$$

$$
\nu \sin \theta = \nu^ {\prime} \sin \theta^ {\prime}\tag{3}
$$

式(1)就是要证明的第二个式子.

将式(1)代入式(2)、(3)，可得12.2.13题要证明的两个式子.

将洛伦兹变换

$$
x = \frac {x ^ {\prime} + v t ^ {\prime}}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}, \quad y = y ^ {\prime}, \quad t = \frac {t ^ {\prime} + \frac {v}{c ^ {2}} x ^ {\prime}}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}
$$

代入相位是不变量式子的左边,再与右边比较 $t'$ 的系数,可得要证明的第一个式子.

12.4.20 以 $\beta = \frac{v}{c} = \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}$ 运动的、每秒钟流量 $10^{6}$ 的 $\mathrm{K}_l^0$ 介子束与铅块相互作用，反应为

$$
\mathbf {K} _ {l} ^ {0} + \text { 铅块 } \rightarrow \mathbf {K} _ {s} ^ {0} + \text { 铅块 }
$$

反应前后铅块的内部状态是相同的,入射的 $K_{l}^{0}$ 与出射的 $K_{s}^{0}$ 的运动方向也是一样的.用

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m (\mathrm{K} _ {l}) = 5 \times 1 0 ^ {8} \mathrm{eV} / c ^ {2} \\ & m (\mathrm{K} _ {l}) - m (\mathrm{K} _ {s}) = 3. 5 \times 1 0 ^ {- 6} \mathrm{eV} / c ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

求出因为这一过程作用在铅块上的平均力的大小和方向(以达因或牛顿为单位).

解 分别用 $m_{l}, m_{s}$ 表示 $m(\mathrm{K}_{l}), m(\mathrm{K}_{s})$ ，一个入射的 $K_{l}$ 介子的能量和动量分别为

$$
\begin{array}{l} E _ {l} = m _ {l} \gamma c ^ {2} = \frac {m _ {l} c ^ {2}}{\sqrt {1 - \frac {1}{2}}} = \sqrt {2} m _ {l} c ^ {2} \\ p _ {l} = m _ {l} \gamma \beta c = \sqrt {2} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} m _ {l} c = m _ {l} c \end{array}
$$

因反应前后铅块的内部状态相同,铅块的质量比介子束大得多,可以认为铅块不动,介子在反应前后能量应是相同的,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & E _ {s} = E _ {l} \\ & p _ {s} ^ {2} c ^ {2} = E _ {s} ^ {2} - m _ {s} ^ {2} c ^ {4} = E _ {l} ^ {2} - m _ {s} ^ {2} c ^ {4} \\ & \quad = c ^ {2} p _ {l} ^ {2} + m _ {l} ^ {2} c ^ {4} - m _ {s} ^ {2} c ^ {4} = 2 m _ {l} ^ {2} c ^ {4} - m _ {s} ^ {2} c ^ {4} \\ & \quad \approx m _ {l} ^ {2} c ^ {4} + 2 m _ {l} (m _ {l} - m _ {s}) c ^ {4} \\ & p _ {s} c = m _ {l} c ^ {2} \left[ 1 + \frac {2 (m _ {l} - m _ {s})}{m _ {l}} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \approx m _ {l} c ^ {2} + (m _ {l} - m _ {s}) c ^ {2} \\ & \quad p _ {s} - p _ {l} = (m _ {l} - m _ {s}) c \end{array}
$$

由于相互作用,介子束每秒钟动量的增量为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (p _ {s} - p _ {l}) \times 1 0 ^ {6} & = (m _ {l} - m _ {s}) c \times 1 0 ^ {6} \\ & = \frac {3 . 5 \times 1 0 ^ {- 6} \times 1 . 6 \times 1 0 ^ {- 1 9} \times 3 \times 1 0 ^ {8} \times 1 0 ^ {6}}{(3 \times 1 0 ^ {8}) ^ {2}} = 1. 8 7 \times 1 0 ^ {- 2 7} (\mathrm{N}) \end{array}
$$

这是铅块作用于介子束上的平均力,沿介子运动方向,可见介子作用于铅块的力,大小为 $1.87\times10^{-27}N$ ,方向与介子束运动方向相反.

12.4.21 一个静止质量为 $m$ 、荷电 $q$ 的粒子，在一均匀磁场 $\pmb{B} = B\pmb{k}$ 中以半径 $R$ 在 $xy$ 平面中做圆周运动.

(1) 求 B 与 q、R、m 及角频率 $\omega$ 的关系；

(2) 因为磁场 B 对粒子不做功, 粒子的速率为常数, 但另一以匀速 Vi 运动的观察者却看到粒子的速率不是常数, 这个观察者测得的 $u_{4}^{\prime}$ [粒子四维速度的第四分量 (粒子的四维速度矢量定义为 $\gamma_{u}u, \gamma_{u}c$ )] 是什么样的?

(3) 计算 $\frac{\mathrm{d}u_4'}{\mathrm{d}\tau}, \frac{\mathrm{d}p_4'}{\mathrm{d}\tau}$ , 在观察者看来, 粒子的能量怎样改变?

解（1）在实验室参考系(S 系)中,动力学方程为

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} \pmb {p}}{\mathrm{d} t} = q \pmb {u} \times \pmb {B}
$$

因 $p \parallel \dot{u}$ ,

$$
\boldsymbol {p} \cdot \frac {\mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {p}}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {1}{2} \frac {\mathrm{d} p ^ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t} = (q \boldsymbol {u} \times \boldsymbol {B}) \cdot \boldsymbol {p} = 0
$$

p 的大小不变, 因而 u 的大小不变,

$$
\gamma_ {u} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {u}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}
$$

为常量. 因 $p = m\gamma_{u} u$ ，动力学方程可改写为

$$
\gamma_ {u} m \frac {\mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {u}}{\mathrm{d} t} = q \boldsymbol {u} \times \boldsymbol {B}
$$

代入 $u=\dot{x}i+\dot{y}j,\quad B=Bk,$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \gamma_ {u} m \ddot {x} = q B \dot {y} \\ & \gamma_ {u} m \ddot {y} = - q B \dot {x} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

(2) 

式(1)加式(2)乘i,并令 $\xi=\dot{x}+iy$ ,得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \dot {\xi} = - \mathrm{i} \frac {q B}{m \gamma_ {u}} \xi \\ & \xi = \xi_ {0} \exp \left(- \mathrm{i} \frac {q B}{m \gamma_ {u}} t\right) \end{array}
$$

$\xi$ 和 $\xi_0$ 均为复数，设 $\xi_0 = |\xi_0|e^{-i\alpha}$ ，则

$$
\dot {x} = \operatorname{Re} \xi = | \xi_ {0} | \cos \left(\frac {q B}{m \gamma_ {u}} t + \alpha\right)
$$

$$
\dot {y} = \operatorname{Im} \xi = - | \xi_ {0} | \sin \left(\frac {q B}{m \gamma_ {u}} t + \alpha\right)
$$

$$
x = x _ {0} + | \xi_ {0} | \frac {m \gamma_ {u}}{q B} \sin \left(\frac {q B}{m \gamma_ {u}} t + \alpha\right) = x _ {0} + R \sin (\omega t + \alpha)
$$

$$
y = y _ {0} + \left| \xi_ {0} \right| \frac {m \gamma_ {u}}{q B} \cos \left(\frac {q B}{m \gamma_ {u}} t + \alpha\right) = y _ {0} + R \cos (\omega t + \alpha)
$$

其中

$$
\omega = \frac {q B}{m \gamma_ {u}}, \quad R = | \xi_ {0} | \frac {m \gamma_ {u}}{q B}, \quad | \xi_ {0} | = R \omega
$$

$$
B = \frac {1}{q} m \gamma_ {u} \omega = \frac {1}{q} m \omega \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \frac {u ^ {2}}{c ^ {2}}}}
$$

因为

$$
u = \sqrt {\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}} = | \xi_ {0} | = R \omega
$$

所以

$$
B = \frac {m \omega}{q \sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {R \omega}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}
$$

(2) 四维速度矢量定义为

$$
u _ {\mu} = (\gamma_ {u} u _ {x}, \gamma_ {u} u _ {y}, \gamma_ {u} u _ {z}, \gamma_ {u} c)
$$

设 $S, S'$ 系分别为实验室参考系和随观察者运动的参考系，由洛伦兹变换关系.

$$
u _ {4} ^ {\prime} = \gamma u _ {4} - \gamma \frac {v}{c} u _ {1}
$$

其中

$$
\gamma = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {v}{c}\right) ^ {2}}}
$$

即

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \gamma_ {u} ^ {\prime} c & = \gamma \left(\gamma_ {u} c - \frac {v}{c} u _ {1}\right) \\ & = \gamma \left(\gamma_ {u} c - \frac {v}{c} \gamma_ {u} \dot {x}\right) \\ & = \gamma \gamma_ {u} \left[ c - \frac {v}{c} R \cos (\omega t + \alpha) \right] \end{array}
$$

将 $t$ 换成固有时 $t = \gamma_{u}\tau (\tau$ 为固有时），则

$$
u _ {4} ^ {\prime} = \gamma_ {u} ^ {\prime} c = \gamma \gamma_ {u} \left[ c - \frac {v}{c} R \omega \cos (\omega \gamma_ {u} \tau + \alpha) \right]
$$

还需将 $\gamma_{u}$ 也写成已知量 m、q、R、B 的函数，

$$
u ^ {2} = (R \omega) ^ {2} = R ^ {2} \bigg (\frac {q B}{m} \bigg) ^ {2} \bigg (1 - \frac {u ^ {2}}{c ^ {2}} \bigg)
$$

$$
u ^ {2} = \frac {(R q B C) ^ {2}}{(m c) ^ {2} + (R q B) ^ {2}}
$$

$$
\gamma_ {u} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {1 - \frac {u ^ {2}}{c ^ {2}}}} = \frac {1}{m c} \sqrt {(m c) ^ {2} + (R q B) ^ {2}}
$$

$$
u _ {4} ^ {\prime} = \frac {\gamma}{m} \left[ \sqrt {(m c) ^ {2} + (R q B) ^ {2}} - \frac {v}{c} R q B \cos \left(\frac {q B}{m} \tau + \alpha\right) \right]
$$

其中 $\gamma = \frac{1}{\sqrt{1 - \left(\frac{v}{c}\right)^2}}$ 

(3) 

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} u _ {4} ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = \frac {\gamma v}{c} R \left(\frac {q B}{m}\right) ^ {2} \sin \left(\frac {q B}{m} \tau + \alpha\right)
$$

因为

$$
p _ {4} ^ {\prime} = \frac {E ^ {\prime}}{c} = \frac {m \gamma_ {u} ^ {\prime} c ^ {2}}{c} = m u _ {4} ^ {\prime}
$$

所以

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} p _ {4} ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = m \frac {\mathrm{d} u _ {4} ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = \frac {\gamma v R}{m c} (q B) ^ {2} \sin \left(\frac {q B}{m} \tau + \alpha\right)
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} E ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = c \frac {\mathrm{d} p _ {4} ^ {\prime}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = \frac {\gamma v R}{m} (q B) ^ {2} \sin \left(\frac {q B}{m} \tau + \alpha\right).
$$

在 S 系中只有磁场, 没有电场, 因而粒子的能量守恒, 在 $S'$ 系中既有磁场, 又有电场, 因而粒子的能量不守恒.

12.4.22 一个静止质量为 $m$ 、荷电 $e$ 的粒子在恒定的电磁场中运动，在某一惯性参考系中， $E = ai, B = bk$ . 阐明粒子的四维速度作为固有时的函数的微分方程，证明方程的解是指数函数的叠加，确定这些指数，在什么条件下（关于 $\pmb{E}$ 和 $\pmb{B}$ ）四维速度的所有分量沿其轨道是有界的.

解 在三维空间的运动微分方程为

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} (m \gamma v)}{\mathrm{d} t} = F\tag{1}
$$

两边点乘 $m\gamma c^2 v$ 

因为

$$
\boldsymbol {p} = m \gamma \boldsymbol {v}
$$

所以

$$
\frac {1}{2} c ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm{d} p ^ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t} = m \gamma c ^ {2} \boldsymbol {F} \cdot \boldsymbol {v}
$$

因为

$$
E ^ {2} = c ^ {2} p ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {4}
$$

所以

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} E ^ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t} = 2 E \frac {\mathrm{d} E}{\mathrm{d} t} = c ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm{d} p ^ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t}
$$

又有

$$
E = m \gamma c ^ {2}
$$

$$
E \frac {\mathrm{d} E}{\mathrm{d} t} = \frac {1}{2} c ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm{d} p ^ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t} = m \gamma c ^ {2} \boldsymbol {F} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} = E \boldsymbol {F} \cdot \boldsymbol {v}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} E}{\mathrm{d} t} = \boldsymbol {F} \cdot \boldsymbol {v}
$$

即

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} (m \gamma c ^ {2})}{\mathrm{d} t} = \boldsymbol {F} \cdot \boldsymbol {v}\tag{2}
$$

用lu、IF分别表示四维速度矢量和四维力矢量，

$$
\mathbb {I} \mathbf {u} = (\gamma \boldsymbol {v}, \gamma_ {c}), \quad \mathbb {I F} = (\gamma \boldsymbol {F}, \gamma \frac {\boldsymbol {F} \cdot \boldsymbol {v}}{c})
$$

采用固有时, $dt=\gamma d\tau$ ,式(1)可改写为

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} (m \gamma v)}{\gamma \mathrm{d} \tau} = F, \quad m \frac {\mathrm{d} (\gamma v)}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = \gamma F
$$

式(2)可改写为

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} (m \gamma c ^ {2})}{\gamma \mathrm{d} \tau} = F \cdot v, \quad m \frac {\mathrm{d} (\gamma c)}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = \gamma \frac {E \cdot v}{c}
$$

改写后的式(1)、(2)可合写为四维速度关于固有时的微分方程

$$
m \frac {\mathrm{d} \mathbf {l u}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = \mathbb {1 F}\tag{3}
$$

今

$$
\boldsymbol {F} = e (\boldsymbol {E} + \boldsymbol {v} \times \boldsymbol {B}) = e (a + b v _ {y}) \boldsymbol {i} - e b v _ {x} \boldsymbol {j}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {F} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} = e (\boldsymbol {E} + \boldsymbol {v} \times \boldsymbol {B}) \cdot \boldsymbol {v} = e \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} = e a v _ {x}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} I F & = (\gamma F, \gamma \frac {F \cdot v}{c}) \\ & = (\gamma e (a + b v _ {y}), - \gamma e b v _ {x}, 0, \gamma \frac {e a}{c} v _ {x}) \\ & = (\frac {e a}{c} u _ {4} + e b u _ {2}, - e b u _ {1}, 0, \frac {e a}{c} u _ {1}) \end{array}
$$

将上述 $\mathbb{F}$ 代入式(3)，写成分量方程，

$$
m \frac {\mathrm{d} u _ {1}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = \frac {e a}{c} u _ {4} + e b u _ {2}
$$

$$
m \frac {\mathrm{d} u _ {2}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = - e b u _ {1}
$$

$$
m \frac {\mathrm{d} u _ {3}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = 0
$$

$$
m \frac {\mathrm{d} u _ {4}}{\mathrm{d} \tau} = \frac {e a}{c} u _ {1}
$$

$u_{3}$ 是常量. 令 $u_{j} = A_{j}\mathrm{e}^{\lambda x}, (j = 1,2,4)$ , 代入微分方程组, 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m \lambda A _ {1} - e b A _ {2} - \frac {e a}{c} A _ {4} = 0 \\ & \qquad e b A _ {1} + m \lambda A _ {2} = 0 \\ & \qquad - \frac {e a}{c} A _ {1} + m \lambda A _ {4} = 0 \end{array}
$$

$A_{1}, A_{2}, A_{4}$ 不全为零，必须其系数行列式为零.

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c c} m \lambda & - e b & - \frac {e a}{c} \\ e b & m \lambda & 0 \\ - \frac {e a}{c} & 0 & m \lambda \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

即

$$
m \lambda \left(m ^ {2} \lambda^ {2} + e ^ {2} b ^ {2} - \frac {e ^ {2} a ^ {2}}{c ^ {2}}\right) = 0
$$

解出

$$
\lambda_ {1} = 0, \quad \lambda_ {2} = \frac {e}{m c} \sqrt {a ^ {2} - c ^ {2} b ^ {2}}, \quad \lambda_ {3} = - \frac {e}{m c} \sqrt {a ^ {2} - c ^ {2} b ^ {2}}
$$

四维速度各分量除 $u_{3}$ 为常量外，均是以 $\lambda_1, \lambda_2, \lambda_3$ 为指数函数的线性叠加，所有分量作为固有时的函数均有界要求 $\lambda_1, \lambda_2, \lambda_3$ 为虚数或零，由此要求：

$$
| a | \leqslant | c b | \quad \text {即} \quad | E | \leqslant c | B |
$$

12.4.23 考虑图 12.32 所示的反应, 其中静止质量为 $m_{1}$ 和 $m_{2}$ 的粒子是入射粒子, 而静止质量为 $m_{3}$ 和 $m_{4}$ 的粒子是出射粒子, $\mathbb{k}, \mathbb{I}\mathbb{p}$ 是它们的四维动量, $\mathbb{I}\mathbb{k}$ 的定义为 $\mathbb{I}\mathbb{k} = \left(k, \mathrm{i}\frac{E}{c}\right)$ , 下面给出的变量常用来描述这种类型的反应.

$$
s = - \left(\mathbb {1} \mathrm{k} _ {1} + \mathbb {1 p} _ {1}\right) ^ {2}, t = - \left(\mathbb {1} \mathrm{k} _ {1} - \mathbb {1 k} _ {2}\right) ^ {2}, u = - \left(\mathbb {1} \mathrm{k} _ {1} - \mathbb {1 p} _ {2}\right) ^ {2}
$$

$(\mathbb{1}\mathbf{k}_1 + \mathbb{1}\mathbf{p}_1)^2$ 是 $(\mathbb{1}\mathbf{k}_1 + \mathbb{1}\mathbf{p}_1) \cdot (\mathbb{1}\mathbf{k}_1 + \mathbb{1}\mathbf{p}_1)$ 的缩写.

(1) 证明: $s + t + u = \sum_{i=1}^{4} m_i^2 c^2$ ;

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/71df54ee-e286-495d-894a-26de614f5c7d/57f6f04db808b6d599be6e3f69af4d02962bf9ab6070b31da98412e78cad152b.jpg)



图12.32


(2) 假设反应是弹性散射, 并令 $m_{1} = m_{3} = \mu, m_{2} = m_{4} = m$ , 在动量中心参考系中, 令静止质量为 $\mu$ 的粒子的入射和出射的三维动量分别为 $\pmb{k}$ 和 $\pmb{k}'$ , 尽可能简单地用 $\pmb{k}, \pmb{k}'$ 将 $s, t, u$ 表示出来, 说明 $s, t, u$ 的物理意义;

(3) 假定在实验室参考系中, (2) 向中所述静止质量为 $m$ 的粒子初态是静止的, 将静止质量为 $\mu$ 的粒子在实验室参考系中的初能量、末能量及散射角用 $s, t, u$ 表示出来.

解 (1) $\mathbb{I}\mathbf{k}^2, \mathbb{I}\mathfrak{p}^2$ 均是洛伦兹不变量, 等于在其静止参考系

中的值,即

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} ^ {2} & = - m _ {1} c ^ {2}, \mathbb {1 k} _ {2} ^ {2} = - m _ {3} c ^ {2}, \mathbb {1 p} _ {1} ^ {2} = - m _ {2} c ^ {2}, \mathbb {1 p} _ {2} ^ {2} = - m _ {4} c ^ {2} \\ s + t + u & = - (\mathbb {1 k} _ {1} + \mathbb {1 p} _ {1}) ^ {2} - (\mathbb {1 k} _ {1} - \mathbb {1 k} _ {2}) ^ {2} - (\mathbb {1 k} _ {1} - \mathbb {1 p} _ {2}) ^ {2} \\ & = - \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} ^ {2} - \mathbb {1 p} _ {1} ^ {2} - 2 \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} \cdot \mathbb {1 p} _ {1} - \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} ^ {2} - \mathbb {1 k} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & + 2 \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} \cdot \mathbb {1 k} _ {2} - \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} ^ {2} - \mathbb {1 p} _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} \cdot \mathbb {1 p} _ {2} \\ & = - (\mathbb {1 k} _ {1} ^ {2} + \mathbb {1 k} _ {2} ^ {2} + \mathbb {1 p} _ {1} ^ {2} + \mathbb {1 p} _ {2} ^ {2}) - 2 \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} \cdot (\mathbb {1 k} _ {1} - \mathbb {1 k} _ {2} + \mathbb {1 p} _ {1} - \mathbb {1 p} _ {2}) \\ & = m _ {1} ^ {2} c ^ {2} + m _ {3} ^ {2} c ^ {2} + m _ {2} ^ {2} c ^ {2} + m _ {4} ^ {2} c ^ {4} - 2 \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} \cdot (\mathbb {1 k} _ {1} + \mathbb {1 p} _ {1} - \mathbb {1 k} _ {2} - \mathbb {1 p} _ {2}) \end{array}
$$

因为四维动量在反应中守恒，

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbb {1 k} _ {1} + \mathbb {1 p} _ {1} = \mathbb {1 k} _ {2} + \mathbb {1 p} _ {2} \\ s + t + u = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {4} m _ {i} ^ {2} c ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

(2) 静止质量为 $\mu$ 和 $m$ 的两粒子发生弹性散射, 散射前后四维动量分别为 $\mathbf{k}, \mathbf{l}\mathbf{p}$ 和 $\mathbf{l}\mathbf{k}'$ , $\mathbf{l}\mathbf{p}'$ .

在动量中心参考系中，在散射前后三维动量不仅守恒，且系统动量均为零，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {k} + \pmb {p} = \pmb {k} ^ {\prime} + \pmb {p} ^ {\prime} = 0} \\ & {\mathbb {1 k} = (\pmb {k}, \mathrm{i} \sqrt {k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}), \quad \mathbb {1 p} = (- \pmb {k}, \mathrm{i} \sqrt {k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2}})} \\ & {\mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime} = (\pmb {k} ^ {\prime}, \mathrm{i} \sqrt {k ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}), \quad \mathbb {1 p} ^ {\prime} = (- \pmb {k} ^ {\prime}, \mathrm{i} \sqrt {k ^ {\prime 2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2}})} \\ & {s = - (\mathbb {1 k} + \mathbb {1 p}) ^ {2} = - (\mathbb {1 k} ^ {2} + \mathbb {1 p} ^ {2} + 2 \mathbb {1 k} \cdot \mathbb {1 p})} \\ & {\qquad = - [ k ^ {2} - (k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) ] - [ k ^ {2} - (k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2}) ]} \\ & {\qquad - 2 [ - k ^ {2} - \sqrt {(k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) (k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2})} ]} \\ & {\qquad = \mu^ {2} c ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2} + 2 k ^ {2} + \sqrt {(k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) (k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2})}} \\ & {t = - (\mathbb {1 k} - \mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime}) ^ {2} = - (\mathbb {1 k} ^ {2} + \mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime 2} - 2 \mathbb {1 k} \cdot \mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime})} \\ & {\qquad = - [ k ^ {2} - (k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) ] - [ k ^ {\prime 2} - (k ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) ]} \\ & {\qquad + 2 [ \pmb {k} \cdot \pmb {k} ^ {\prime} - \sqrt {(k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) (k ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2})} ]} \\ & {\qquad = 2 \mu^ {2} c ^ {2} + 2 \pmb {k} \cdot \pmb {k} ^ {\prime} - 2 \sqrt {(k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) (k ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2})}} \\ & {\qquad u = - (\mathbb {1 k} - \mathbb {1 p} ^ {\prime}) ^ {2} = - (\mathbb {1 k} ^ {2} + \mathbb {1 p} ^ {\prime 2} - 2 \mathbb {1 k} \cdot \mathbb {1 p} ^ {\prime})} \\ & {\qquad = - [ k ^ {2} - (k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) ] - [ k ^ {\prime 2} - (k ^ {\prime 2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2}) ]} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & + 2 \left[ - k \cdot k ^ {\prime} - \sqrt {(k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) (k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2})} \right] \\ & = \mu^ {2} c ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2} - 2 k \cdot k ^ {\prime} - 2 \sqrt {(k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) (k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2})} \end{array}
$$

为了阐明 s、t 和 u 的物理意义, 改写为

$$
s = \left(\sqrt {k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}} + \sqrt {k ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {1}{c ^ {2}} \left(\sqrt {k ^ {2} c ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {4}} + \sqrt {k ^ {2} c ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {4}}\right) ^ {2}
$$

可见, $c^{2}s$ 是在动量中心参考系中做弹性散射的两粒子系统的总能量的平方.

$$
t = - \left(\boldsymbol {k} ^ {\prime} - \boldsymbol {k}\right) ^ {2} + \left(\sqrt {\boldsymbol {k} ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}} - \sqrt {\boldsymbol {k} ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}\right) ^ {2}
$$

t 是在两粒子弹性散射时, 静止质量为 $\mu$ 的粒子在弹性散射过程中四维动量的增量的平方的负值.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} u & = - (k ^ {\prime} + k) ^ {2} + \left(\sqrt {k ^ {\prime 2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2}} - \sqrt {k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \\ & = - (- k ^ {\prime} - k) ^ {2} + \left(\sqrt {k ^ {\prime 2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2}} - \sqrt {k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

u 是静止质量不同的两粒子在弹性散射前后四维动量的改变量的平方的负值.

其实,从定义式即能说明 t、u 这样的物理意义.

s、t、u 是粒子物理中处理两粒子弹性碰撞常用的具有洛伦兹不变性的量,称为孟德斯坦变量.

(3) 两粒子发生弹性散射, p=0,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \mathbb {1} \mathbf {k} & = (\boldsymbol {k}, \mathrm{i} \sqrt {k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}), \quad \mathbb {1 p} = (\mathbf {0}, \mathrm{i} m c) \\ \mathbb {1} \mathbf {k} ^ {\prime} & = (\boldsymbol {k} ^ {\prime}, \mathrm{i} \sqrt {k ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}) \end{array}
$$

由四维动量守恒，

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\mathbb {1 k} + \mathfrak {l p} = \mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime} + \mathfrak {l p} ^ {\prime}, \quad \mathbb {1 k} - \mathfrak {l p} ^ {\prime} = \mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime} - \mathfrak {l p}} \\ & {s = - (\mathbb {1 k} + \mathfrak {l p}) ^ {2} = - \mathbb {1 k} ^ {2} - \mathfrak {l p} ^ {2} - 2 \mathbb {1 k} \cdot \mathfrak {l p}} \\ & {\quad = - [ k ^ {2} - (k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) ] - (- m ^ {2} c ^ {2}) - 2 m c \sqrt {k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}} \\ & {\quad = \mu^ {2} c ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2} - 2 m E _ {\mu}} \\ & {\qquad E _ {\mu} = \frac {(\mu^ {2} + m ^ {2}) c ^ {2} - s}{2 m}} \\ & {u = - (\mathbb {1 k} - \mathfrak {l p} ^ {\prime}) ^ {2} = - (\mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime} - \mathfrak {l p}) ^ {2} = - \mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime 2} - \mathfrak {l p} ^ {2} + 2 \mathbb {1 k} ^ {\prime} \cdot \mathfrak {l p}} \\ & {\quad = - [ k ^ {\prime 2} - (k ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}) ] - (- m ^ {2} c ^ {2}) + 2 (- m c \sqrt {k ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}})} \\ & {\quad = \mu^ {2} c ^ {2} + m ^ {2} c ^ {2} - 2 m E _ {\mu} ^ {\prime}} \\ & {\qquad E _ {\mu} ^ {\prime} = \frac {(\mu^ {2} + m ^ {2}) c ^ {2} - u}{2 m}} \\ & {\qquad t = - (\mathbb {1 k} - \mathfrak {l k} ^ {\prime}) ^ {2} = - \mathbb {1 k} ^ {2} - \mathfrak {l k} ^ {\prime 2} + 2 \mathbb {1 k} \cdot \mathfrak {l k} ^ {\prime}} \\ & {\qquad = 2 \mu^ {2} c ^ {2} + 2 k \cdot k ^ {\prime} - 2 \sqrt {k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}} \cdot \sqrt {k ^ {\prime 2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad = 2 \mu^ {2} c ^ {2} + 2 k k ^ {\prime} \cos \theta - \frac {2}{c ^ {2}} E _ {\mu} E _ {\mu} ^ {\prime}} \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {E _ {k} ^ {2} = c ^ {2} k ^ {2} + \mu^ {2} c ^ {4}, \quad k = \frac {1}{c} \sqrt {E _ {\mu} ^ {2} - \mu^ {2} c ^ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad \cos \theta = \frac {1}{2 k k ^ {\prime}} \Big (t - 2 \mu^ {2} c ^ {2} + \frac {2}{c ^ {2}} E _ {\mu} E _ {\mu} ^ {\prime} \Big)} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {c ^ {2} t - 2 \mu^ {2} c ^ {4} + 2 E _ {\mu} E _ {\mu} ^ {\prime}}{2 \sqrt {(E _ {\mu} ^ {2} - \mu^ {2} c ^ {4}) (E _ {\mu} ^ {\prime} - \mu^ {2} c ^ {4})}}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\cos \theta = \frac {2 m ^ {2} c ^ {2} t - 4 m ^ {2} \mu^ {2} c ^ {4} + [ (\mu^ {2} + m ^ {2}) c ^ {2} - s ] [ (\mu^ {2} + m ^ {2}) c ^ {2} - u ]}{\sqrt {[ (\mu + m) ^ {2} c ^ {2} - s ] [ (\mu - m) ^ {2} c ^ {2} - s ] [ (\mu + m) ^ {2} c ^ {2} - u ] [ (\mu - m) ^ {2} c ^ {2} - u ]}}
$$

将 $E_{\mu}$ 与 $s$ 的关系， $E_{\mu}^{\prime}$ 与 $u$ 的关系代入上式，即得 $\cos \theta$ 与 $s, t, u$ 的关系，经计算可得

物理学大题典

20年磨一剑 30位资深教授合作的结晶
150位高才生的灵性显现

《物理学大题典》是中国科学技术大学30位资深教授长期鼎力合作的成果。这套大型大学物理题解丛书的前身是在中国物理学界久负盛名的《美国物理试题与解答》丛书。《美国物理试题与解答》是CUSPEA项目的成果，内容主要是美国名牌大学研究生院的入学试题解答。本次修订工作历时近2年，丛书除继续涵盖力、热、光、电、近代物理到四大力学全部基础物理学内容之外，还包括了原子核物理、粒子物理、凝聚态物理、等离子体物理、天体物理、激光物理、量子光学和量子信息物理等内容，大体涵盖了当前综合性大学全部本科物理课程的内容。本次修订补充了近年美国的考题、中国的考题和俄罗斯等国的考题，也有一些题目源自编委们的科研工作成果。

《物理学大题典》以先进的教育理念为指导，注重物理、注重学科交叉、注重与科研结合，富于“当代感”，是物理系师生的必备工具书。

力学下册 1

①《力学》 ②《电磁学与电动力学》 ③《光学》
④《原子亚原子与相对论物理学》 ⑤《热学 热力学 统计物理》
⑥《量子力学》 ⑦《固体物理及物理量测量》
